Addressing this limitation, we introduce the Logit Refiner, a lightweight autoregressive module that restores intra-scale dependencies by sequentially sampling tokens conditioned on frozen backbone features. Adding only ~10% parameters and less than 5% of the base model's training compute, it plugs into any pretrained VAR checkpoint without retraining. Controlled ablations isolate joint intra-scale sampling -- rather than additional capacity or training -- as the critical ingredient. Across backbones from 310M to 2B parameters on class-conditional ImageNet 256x256, the refiner consistently improves generation quality, enabling a 1.1B-parameter model to surpass one twice its size. The approach further generalizes to text-to-image generation, confirming that the mean-field bottleneck persists across VAR variants and is effectively alleviated by our method.
  Project page: https://compvis.github.io/logit-refiner/

</details>


### [72] [Guided Super-Resolution of Digital Elevation Models with Diffusion-Based Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11886)
*Armand Mihai Nicolicioiu,Dominik Narnhofer,Nando Metzger,Daniel Panangian,Ksenia Bittner,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于去噪扩散的引导式超分辨率方法，将商业卫星5米DSM提升至0.5米分辨率，利用高分辨率光谱影像引导，把仅存在于影像中的锐利轮廓与屋顶结构信息迁移到高程图中，在中欧多城市实验中获得结构细节与几何精度俱佳的高分辨率DSM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率DSM在城市分析、三维建筑重建与基础设施监测中至关重要，但获取成本高、流程复杂；而商业卫星粗DSM与航空/卫星高分辨率光学影像则普遍可得，由此产生空间分辨率不匹配的问题，需要一种方法在仅有粗高程数据与高分影像的条件下重建精细高程。

Method: 采用去噪扩散模型实现引导式DSM超分辨率：以5 m DSM为条件、以高分辨率光谱影像作为引导，在扩散过程中将影像中可见的锐利轮廓与屋顶细节等结构信息注入高程图，从而完成从5 m到0.5 m的跨模态分辨率提升，区别于传统插值与滤波方法。

Result: 在中欧多个城市上的实验表明，方法生成的高分辨率DSM具有更好的结构细节与准确的表面几何，重建精度优于常规插值与滤波技术。

Conclusion: 结果说明，结合基础图像先验的引导式超分辨率是重建高分辨率地表模型的有效途径，可缓解高分辨率DSM数据稀缺问题。

Abstract: High-resolution digital surface models (DSMs) play an important role in urban analysis, 3D building reconstruction, and infrastructure monitoring, yet their availability remains limited due to the high cost and complexity of data acquisition. In contrast, coarse DSMs from commercial satellite missions are widely accessible, and high-resolution optical imagery is increasingly available from aerial and satellite platforms. We address the resulting mismatch in spatial resolution and propose a DSM superresolution approach that enhances 5 m DSMs to 0.5 m resolution, using guidance from high-resolution spectral images. Our method employs denoising diffusion to transfer information that is visible only in the image, like crisp outlines and detailed roof structures, into the elevation maps. In this way, surface details are reconstructed more accurately than with conventional interpolation or filtering techniques. Experiments on several cities in Central Europe demonstrate that the proposed approach produces high-quality DSMs with improved structural detail and accurate surface geometry. Our results highlight the potential of guided super-resolution with foundational image priors as a means of reconstructing high-resolution surface models.

</details>


### [73] [3D Point Splatting for mmWave Radar Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11894)
*Adnan Armouti,Yixuan Gao,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3D Point Splatting（3DPS），首个直接从雷达方程立体角形式推导的可微点渲染器，每个3D点携带ITU-R P.2040材质模型并以闭式求解，其复相位通过预计算PSF溅射到距离bin，实现相位保持、材质显式建模与多视角可训练的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达新视角合成（NVS）需要同时满足物理忠实、复数值输出与多视角可训练三个属性的渲染器，但现有方法无法三者兼得：可微蒙特卡洛光线追踪物理忠实且输出复数，却无法扩展到NVS所需的多视角优化；NeRF/哈希网格/3D高斯等光学NVS移植方案训练快，但丢弃相位、用不透明学习特征替代显式材质建模，只能输出功率域的RA幅度。

Method: 3DPS从雷达方程的标准立体角形式直接推导可微点渲染器：每个有向3D点携带ITU-R P.2040材质模型并闭式求值，产生复相位；该复相位经预计算点扩散函数（PSF）溅射进距离bin。输出为复数，因而渲染器与下游格式无关，同一优化场景可通过标准FFT管线产生ADC、复距离剖面（CRP）与RA三种输出，无需按格式分别重训练。

Result: 在六个室外ColoRadar场景上，3DPS在留出RA图像上取得0.587的平均Pearson相关系数，是三个光学NVS基线（RadarSplat、Radar Fields、DART）的1.7倍至5.2倍；单张RTX 4090上每场景训练约3分钟。

Conclusion: 3DPS首次在单一可微渲染器中同时实现物理忠实、复数输出与多视角可训练，且训练高效、输出格式无关，为雷达NVS建立了兼具物理一致性与实用性的基线。其价值主要在雷达感知与仿真侧，尚未在机器人闭环或与视觉/语言的融合上验证。

Abstract: Solving novel view synthesis (NVS) for millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar requires a renderer that is physically faithful, complex-valued, and multi-viewpoint-tractable. No prior method achieves these three properties simultaneously. Differentiable Monte Carlo (MC) ray tracers implement the radar forward model directly with explicit material modeling and complex outputs, but do not scale to the multi-view optimization NVS demands. Optical-NVS ports of NeRF, hash grids, and 3D Gaussians train fast but discard phase and replace explicit material modeling with opaque learned features, restricting them to power-only range-azimuth (RA) magnitudes. We propose 3D Point Splatting (3DPS), the first differentiable point renderer for radar, derived directly from the standard solid-angle form of the radar equation. Each oriented 3D point carries an ITU-R P.2040 material model, evaluated in closed form, with the resulting complex phasor splatted into range bins through a precomputed point spread function (PSF). The complex-valued output makes the renderer product-agnostic. The same optimized scene yields analog-to-digital converter (ADC), complex range profile (CRP), and RA outputs through standard fast Fourier transform (FFT) pipelines without retraining for each format. On six outdoor ColoRadar scenes, 3DPS reaches 0.587 mean Pearson correlation on held-out RA images. This is between 1.7x and 5.2x the three optical-NVS baselines (RadarSplat, Radar Fields, DART). Training takes approximately 3 minutes per scene on a single RTX 4090.

</details>


### [74] [Caption-once, Frames-on-Demand: Visual-Need Routing for Budget-Aware Agentic Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11899)
*Weitong Cai,Hang Zhang,Yukai Huang,Yiqiao Xie,Shan Gao,Jiankang Deng,Songcen Xu,Jifei Song,Zhensong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CFD（Caption-once, Frames-onDemand）边云协同智能体框架，用于长视频理解：边缘端离线一次生成双轨叙事索引（事件级故事骨架 + 片段级微日志），查询时云端 MLLM 以“故事优先”循环推理，并由 Visual-Need Router 按查询条件门控关键帧检索，将视觉访问变为可约束的查询级成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备长视频理解需在严格算力与带宽下推理数小时内容。视觉 token 下采样破坏时序结构，纯文本视频记忆又丢失细粒度视觉属性，二者各有短板，需要按需而非全程调用视觉信息。

Method: 利用“视觉-文本对偶性”观察（语言记忆擅长长程时序结构、像素对属性级感知关键），构建预算感知的边云智能体框架。边缘端单次离线 caption 生成双轨叙事索引（事件级故事骨架 + 片段级微日志），缓存复用避免重复 caption；云端 MLLM 以故事优先循环推理，核心是轻量 Visual-Need Router——按查询门控，仅对感知类问题（外观、屏上文字、属性消歧）触发有界关键帧检索，时序结构类问题留在语言空间，从而限制每查询帧消耗且与视频长度无关。

Result: 在长视频基准上取得较强的精度-效率权衡，同时大幅减少在线视觉处理开销。

Conclusion: 通过视觉-文本对偶性与查询条件化的视觉门控，可在边缘端以恒定视觉预算实现长视频理解，验证了“按需取帧”在准确率与效率上的可行性。

Abstract: Long-video understanding on edge devices must reason over hours of content under tight compute and bandwidth budgets. Subsampling visual tokens loses temporal structure, while text-only video memories lose fine-grained visual attributes. We observe a visual-textual duality: language memories carry long-range temporal structure better than dense frames, while pixels remain decisive for attribute-level perception. Building on this insight, we propose Caption-once, Frames-onDemand (CFD), a budget-aware edge-cloud agentic framework. The edge runs a single offline captioning pass that builds a dual-track narrative index, an event-level story skeleton plus a clip-level micro-log, cached and reused across queries without re-captioning. At query time, a cloud-side MLLM reasons over the index in a story-first loop centered on a lightweight Visual-Need Router: a per-query gating module that triggers bounded keyframe retrieval only for perceptual questions (appearance, on-screen text, attribute disambiguation) and keeps temporal-structural questions in language space. The router turns visual access into a first-class, query-conditioned cost, capping per-query frame consumption regardless of video length. Experiments on long-video benchmarks demonstrate strong accuracy-efficiency trade-offs while substantially reducing online visual processing.

</details>


### [75] [SenseNova-U1.5: Towards Native Unified Visual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11929)
*Haiwen Diao,Jiahao Wang,Chenjing Ding,Hanming Deng,Jiangnan Chen,Ruixi Zhang,Ruohui Wang,Wenwen Tong,Xiangyu Fan,Yubo Wang,Yue Zhu,Yuwei Niu,Zhengqi Bai,Zhiqian Lin,Zhitao Yang,Zhongang Cai,Bo Yang,Chen Feng,Chengguang Lv,Guangjia Liu,Guanlin Wang,Hanyu Zhang,Haojia Yu,Hongcan Xiao,Hongli Wang,Huan Wu,Huaping Zhong,Jian Fang,Jianan Fan,Jiaqi Li,Jiefan Lu,Jing Zuo,Jingcheng Ni,Junxiang Xu,Linjun Dai,Mutian Xu,Peishen Yan,Penghao Wu,Ruijie Mao,Ruisi Wang,Shihao Bai,Shuang Yang,Shuya Yang,Shuyan Zheng,Silei Wu,Siying Li,Tao Chu,Tianbo Zhong,Tongxi Zhou,Weichao Luo,Weichen Fan,Wenhao Jia,Wenjie Gao,Xiangli Kong,Yan Li,Yang Yong,Zimo Wen,Zixuan Qian,Wenxiu Sun,Ruihao Gong,Quan Wang,Lewei Lu,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SenseNova-U1.5 是一个 8B 的 MoT 原生统一多模态模型，在无编码器、无 VAE 架构下同时完成理解、推理与图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型多依赖外部视觉编码器与 VAE 潜空间，导致理解与生成之间存在表示鸿沟。作者希望验证"原生统一建模"能否在完全端到端的框架下同时实现感知、推理与创作。

Method: 1) 采用无编码器、无 VAE 的原生视觉接口，通过空间一致的 patch 重建强化视觉表征；2) 扩充高质量生成与编辑数据（含任务形式化改进、结构化提示增强、最高 4K 原生分辨率）；3) 后训练阶段为视觉美学、双语文字渲染、信息图生成、图像编辑分别训练专家，再用多专家 on-policy 蒸馏整合能力。

Result: 在大规模评测中显著提升了图像保真度、文字渲染、复杂构图、多参考编辑与交错生成能力，同时改善指令遵循并保持主体身份、几何一致性与未编辑区域不变。尽管生成数据中结构化格式曝光有限，仍能泛化到长而复杂的结构化视觉指令。

Conclusion: 结果表明，多模态理解能力可迁移至视觉规划与创作，原生统一建模是通向全端到端"感知—推理—创造"系统的可行路径；作者将开源监督微调、强化学习与 on-policy 蒸馏的训练代码。

Abstract: We launch SenseNova-U1.5, an 8B-MoT native unified multimodal model that understands, reasons about, and generates visual content within an encoder-free and VAE-free architecture. We strengthen its visual interface through spatially coherent patch reconstruction and scale its training with carefully curated generation and editing data, improved task formulation, structural prompt enhancement, and native resolutions of up to 4K. For post-training, we optimize specialized experts for visual aesthetics, bilingual text rendering, infographic generation, and image editing, and consolidate their capabilities through multi-expert on-policy distillation. Across extensive evaluations, SenseNova-U1.5 largely advances image fidelity, text rendering, complex composition, multi-reference editing, and interleaved generation, while improving instruction following and preserving subject identity, geometry, and unmodified regions. Despite limited exposure to structured formats in its generation data, SenseNova-U1.5 generalizes effectively to long, complex, and structured visual instructions, further proving that multimodal understanding can transfer to visual planning and creation. Together, these findings position native unified modelling as a promising path towards systems that perceive, reason and create within a fully end-to-end framework. We will open-source training code, including supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

### [76] [HuRo: Robotizing Human Videos for Scalable VLA Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10706)
*Jinho Jeong,Se June Joo,Jaehyun Kang,Dongyun Kim,Yena Kim,Hanjung Kim,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作系统研究了"机器人化"人类视频能否为VLA策略预训练提供有效可扩展监督，构建了跨标注层级推断缺失中间信号的robotization流水线，据此构建含约63万机器人化episode、1.42亿帧的HuRo数据集，并在四项真实操作任务上验证了规模扩展与OOD鲁棒性的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人数据采集成本高，人类视频数据规模大、多样性丰富但存在具身差异（embodiment gap）。现有方法要么在任务匹配设定下做视频机器人化，要么在大规模下分别处理观测对齐与动作对齐，缺乏对"机器人化人类视频能否作为VLA预训练可扩展监督"的系统性检验。

Method: 提出robotization流水线，将异构人类视频转换为机器人对齐的观测与动作轨迹，并在不同标注层级间推断缺失的中间信号（如手部/末端位姿、动作标签）；基于该流水线从五个人类视频源构建HuRo数据集，包含约630K机器人化episode、142M处理帧；随后用这些数据预训练VLA策略并端到端使用retargeted action。

Result: 在四个真实世界操作任务上，随机器人化预训练规模增大，整体完成率从51.5%提升至80.3%；在空间与视觉分布偏移下OOD完成率从34.9%提升至72.2%。消融实验表明视觉机器人化提升OOD鲁棒性，且带retargeted action的端到端预训练优于仅视觉迁移。

Conclusion: 机器人化人类视频可作为VLA预训练的有效且可扩展的监督来源；视觉对齐增益OOD鲁棒性，而动作重定向的端到端预训练是更优的迁移范式。代码与数据已开源，为该方向提供了可复现的基线与数据基础。

Abstract: Human video datasets have emerged as a compelling alternative to expensive real-robot data, offering rich diversity at scale. To bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap, existing approaches either robotize videos in task-matched settings or address observation and action alignment separately at scale. In this work, we systematically examine whether robotized human videos can provide effective and scalable supervision for pretraining vision-language-action (VLA) policies. To this end, we develop a robotization pipeline that converts heterogeneous human videos into robot-aligned observations and action trajectories while inferring missing intermediate signals across annotation levels. Using this pipeline, we construct the HuRo dataset, comprising about 630K robotized episodes and 142M processed frames from five human-video sources. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, increasing robotized pretraining scale improves overall completion from 51.5% to 80.3% and OOD completion under spatial and visual shifts from 34.9% to 72.2%. Ablations further show that visual robotization improves OOD robustness and that end-to-end pretraining with retargeted actions outperforms visual-only transfer. Code and data are released on our website: https://3587jjh.github.io/HuRo.

</details>


### [77] [When Information is Worth the Risk: Behavioral Valuation for Hazardous Robotic Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10726)
*Alkesh K. Srivastava,Aamodh Suresh,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Philip Dames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在危险探索中，把唯一变量设为路径排序的标量目标（估值层），提出基于 Prelec 概率加权、风险增强的 Behavioral Information 目标，形成可解释的保守—激进信息-风险估值族；理论上揭示估值参数造成的高信息/高风险与低信息/低风险路径切换边界及变换后的 Pareto 前沿结构；大规模失败截断栅格世界实验表明仅改变估值即可重塑信息-风险前沿，并可降低危险暴露与机器人损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危险环境探索（不安全地形、辐射、火灾、雷区、结构损伤）中，信息采集本身可能导致任务失败：高信息量路径往往暴露于危险、触发终止并截断后续观测。因此仅依据不确定性最大化选路不足，还需判断降低不确定性是否值得承担该风险。作者希望在保持信念更新、传感器模型、物理风险模型与有限时域信息规划器不变的前提下，仅通过改变路径排序标量目标来研究这一权衡。

Method: 提出"估值层"视角：固定规划栈的其余组件，只替换用于对可行路径排序的标量目标函数。在该框架内引入基于 Prelec 概率加权（probability weighting）的风险增强 Behavioral Information 目标，得到一个从保守到激进、参数可解释的信息-风险估值族。理论分析方面，刻画估值参数如何产生高信息/高风险路径与低信息/低风险路径之间的切换边界，并推导可行探索策略上经变换的 Pareto 前沿结构。实验方面，在失败截断（failure-truncated）的栅格世界中进行大规模评估，与香农信息规划及标准风险感知基线对比。

Result: 大规模失败截断栅格世界实验显示：仅改变估值即可重塑信息-风险前沿。香农信息规划仍是强有力的原始信息基线，而风险感知目标能通过避免截断未来感知的失败来降低危险暴露与机器人损失。风险增强 Behavioral 估值与标准风险感知基线相比具有 Pareto 竞争力，并提供可解释的保守与中间机制。

Conclusion: 结果支持这样一种框架：机器人不仅应推理某动作降低多少不确定性，还应推理该不确定性的降低是否值得所需的冒险。该工作将危险探索中的信息-风险权衡抽象为可插拔的估值层，为在既有规划栈上引入可解释风险偏好提供了通用且轻量的途径。

Abstract: Hazardous robotic exploration requires robots to map spatial risks, such as unsafe terrain, radiation, fire, mines, or structural damage, while operating where collecting information can itself cause failure. A highly informative path may expose the robot to hazards, terminate execution, and prevent future observations. Hazardous exploration therefore requires deciding not only where uncertainty is largest, but when reducing it is worth the risk. This paper introduces a valuation-layer view of this problem. We keep the belief update, sensor model, physical risk model, and finite-horizon informative planner fixed, and change only the scalar objective used to rank feasible paths. Within this framework, we introduce a risk-augmented Behavioral Information objective based on Prelec probability weighting, yielding an interpretable family of conservative-to-aggressive information-risk valuations. Theoretically, we show that valuation parameters create switching boundaries between high-information/high-risk and lower-information/lower-risk paths, and induce a transformed Pareto-frontier structure over feasible exploration policies. Large-scale failure-truncated grid-world experiments show that valuation alone reshapes the information-risk frontier. Shannon information planning remains a strong raw-information baseline, while risk-aware objectives can reduce hazard exposure and robot losses by avoiding failures that truncate future sensing. Risk-augmented Behavioral valuation is Pareto-competitive with standard risk-aware baselines and provides interpretable conservative and intermediate regimes. These results support a framework in which robots reason not only about how much uncertainty an action reduces, but whether that reduction is worth the risk required to obtain it.

</details>


### [78] [Lie-Algebraic Bell Recurrences for Arbitrary-Order Twist Jets and Parallel-Mechanism Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10748)
*Daniel Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出任意阶对偶螺旋运动学框架，统一串联传播、并联机构闭环与刚体平台点场，用Bell多项式组织各阶导数，数值验证至四阶残差<1e-12。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 串联开链与并联闭环的螺旋（screw）运动学通常在低阶（速度/加速度）层面处理，缺乏一个统一、可递推到任意阶且兼容转动与移动副的解析框架；同时高阶运动（jerk、snap）点场与机构闭合并未在同一表示中被严格联系。

Method: 1) 以对偶螺旋为统一变量，将圆柱副设为原生物理模块，转动副和移动副作为特例；2) 对固定关节轴，用普通Bell多项式组织指数因子各阶导数，链内非交换因子保持物理顺序；3) 给出初始系前缀与末端解析协变两种等价公式，表示串联twist jet；4) 并联机构中通过重复Leibniz微分＋Bell多项式组织关节级导数，得到任意阶三角主-被闭环递推：每阶求解同一被动Jacobian，右端仅含主动数据与低阶jet；5) 将平台twist jet精确映射为速度/加速度/jerk/snap场的点无关仿射不变量；6) 在3C链、RR+RRR球腕、Hunt型6-RUS机构上验证至四阶。

Result: 三条算例（通用3C链含非共面轴与非零转动/移动圆柱坐标、RR+RRR球腕、6-RUS）显示：独立数值微分、仿射场求值与分支闭环微分均在四阶内一致，SI单位下残差低于1e-12。

Conclusion: 该纯运动学公式在主动-被动划分正则的位形下有效，为串联与并联机构的高阶（jerk/snap）运动分析与平台点场计算提供了统一、可任意阶递推的解析工具。

Abstract: This paper develops an arbitrary-order kinematic construction that links serial propagation, parallel-mechanism closure, and rigid-platform point fields within one dual screw framework. A cylindrical joint is retained as one native physical block, with revolute and prismatic joints obtained as special cases. For each fixed joint axis, ordinary Bell polynomials organize the derivatives of the exponential factor; across a chain, the noncommuting factors remain in their physical order. Initial-frame prefix and terminal-resolved covariant formulas then produce equivalent representations of the serial twist jet. For a parallel mechanism, repeated Leibniz differentiation, with joint-level derivatives organized by Bell polynomials, yields an arbitrary-order triangular active-passive closure recurrence: the same passive Jacobian is solved at every derivative order at a regular configuration, while the right-hand side contains only prescribed active data and lower-order jets. The resulting platform twist jet is mapped exactly to the point-independent affine invariants of the velocity, acceleration, jerk, and snap fields. The validation is deliberately complementary: a generic 3C chain with noncoplanar axes and nonzero rotational and translational cylindrical coordinates tests ordered serial propagation, an RR+RRR spherical wrist tests active-passive closure, and a Hunt-type 6-RUS mechanism with six active revolute joints tests an independently reconstructed platform jet and its affine fields. Independent differentiation of the rigid motion, evaluation of the affine fields, and the differentiated branch closures all agree through fourth order with residuals below $10^{-12}$ in the corresponding SI units. The formulation is purely kinematic and applies at configurations where the selected active-passive partition is regular.

</details>


### [79] [Expressive Robotic Pianist: Mastering Complex Piano Repertoire with Graph-Mimic and Musical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10844)
*Yanhong Liang,Xianwei Liu,Chaojie Fu,Shaowen Cheng,Yanyan Yuan,Chengwei Zhuo,Xi Chen,Yongbin Jin,Wei Yang,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习与图优化的机器人钢琴演奏框架，结合物理声学模型实现类人指法与力度表达，在感知实验中被非专业听众视为与人类演奏不可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械精度已较好实现，但机器人演奏缺乏人类钢琴家式的自然指法转换与细腻力度控制，机械性与艺术表现之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 强化学习控制框架 + 基于图的指法优化（生成预压/按键指法策略，逼近人类运动模式）+ 物理启发的声学模型（调制按键速度以复现乐谱力度标记）。

Result: 在指法形态相似度和动态速度准确率上显著优于基线；感知测试中优于基线机器人演奏，对非专业听众与人类演奏不可区分；多风格乐段下保持高音符级准确率并具备表现力。

Conclusion: 为机器人超越机械准确、达到类人表现力演奏提供了可行路径，可迁移至其他高灵巧度表达性操作任务。

Abstract: Enabling robots to perform musical instruments with human-level expressivity represents a frontier in bridging the gap between mechanical precision and artistic interpretation. Despite advances in robotic dexterity, replicating the fluid finger transitions and nuanced dynamic control characteristic of human pianists remains a significant challenge. Through a reinforcement learning-based control framework, we demonstrate that a dexterous robotic hand can achieve high-fidelity performance across a diverse piano repertoire. Central to our approach is a graph-based optimization strategy that guides the robot to generate natural pre-press and key-press fingering strategies that closely resemble human movement patterns. To achieve expressive sound production, the control system is coupled with a physics-inspired acoustic model that modulates keypress velocity to accurately reproduce the dynamic variations specified in musical scores. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate that our expressive control model significantly outperforms baseline methods in both finger morphology similarity and dynamic velocity accuracy. In a perceptual test involving participants from diverse listener groups, performances generated by our system are significantly preferred over baseline robotic performances and are indistinguishable from human performances for non-professional audiences. Furthermore, extensive experiments across multiple musical styles confirm that our method maintains high note-level accuracy while achieving expressive performance. Our approach provides a robust pathway for robotic systems to move beyond mere mechanical accuracy, elevating robotic musicianship to a level of expressive performance comparable to human pianists.

</details>


### [80] [ReactHuman: A Physics-Grounded Benchmark for Human-Like Reactive Decision-Making in Embodied Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10895)
*Yizhan Li,Jianxin You,Mengyang Xiong,Yinhuan Chen,Zicheng Zhao,Dekun Wu,Dongqing Zhang,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReactHuman，首个面向类人反应式决策的物理落地基准：让MLLM作为仿真人形机器人"大脑"应对突发家庭危险，含17类事件、1000+可复现场景、240Hz刚体仿真真值，并用五项指标从合理/安全/物理落地三轴评分，执行每步规划产生可观测后果。评测7个MLLM显示反应式安全远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测对直觉物理多为被动视频问答，或聚焦导航、重排等长时程 deliberative 任务，无法衡量模型能否把物理理解转化为即时、安全攸关的动作。突发物理危险反应（接住滑落的盘子、躲开掉落的刀）既是具身智能的有意义测试，也是MLLM作为家用机器人决策核心的硬性部署要求。

Method: 构建ReactHuman基准：MLLM充当仿真人形大脑应对突发家庭危险，覆盖17个事件族、1000+逐位可复现场景，真值来自240Hz刚体仿真、无需人工标注，并含外观与物理相矛盾的对抗物体（泡沫铁砧、钢苹果）。设计五指标评分套件，从合理、安全、物理落地三轴评分，并对每个提交方案进行物理执行以产生可观测后果。评测7个代表性MLLM。

Result: 反应式安全远未解决：模型约每三个危险就误处理一个；行为来自固定倾向而非观察到的场景；信任外观胜过运动；即便动作选对也错过米级拦截点；且这些失败不随模型规模缩小。

Conclusion: ReactHuman提供细粒度诊断与可扩展训练信号，指向物理落地、安全感知的具身智能体；基准已开源发布。

Abstract: Reacting to sudden physical hazards (catching a slipping plate, dodging a falling knife) is both a meaningful test of embodied intelligence and a hard requirement for deploying multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as the decision coreof household robots. Existing evaluations, however, probe intuitive physics passively through question answering over videos, or target deliberate, long-horizon tasks such as navigation and rearrangement; none measure whether a model can turn physical understanding into immediate, safety-critical action. We introduce ReactHuman, the first physics-grounded benchmark for human-like reactive decision-making, in which the evaluated MLLM acts as the brain of a simulated humanoid facing sudden household hazards; it spans 17 event families and over 1,000 bit-for-bit reproducible scenes with exact, annotation-free ground truth derived from 240 Hz rigid-body simulation, including adversarial objects whose appearance contradicts their physics (a foam anvil, a steel apple). We further design a five-metric suite that scores each reaction along three axes: reasonable, safe, and physically grounded. We physically execute every committed plan so that decisions have observable consequences. With this harness we evaluate seven representative MLLMs. Results show that reactive safety is far from solved: models mishandle roughly one hazard in three, act from fixed dispositions rather than the observed scene, trust appearance over motion, and miss interception points at meter scale even when the chosen action is correct; none of these failures shrink with model scale. ReactHuman thus offers both a fine-grained diagnosis and a scalable training signal toward physically grounded, safety-aware embodied agents. The benchmark can be found here: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Alan123/reacthuman-benchmark-scaled

</details>


### [81] [Planning along Differentiable Charts of Constraint Manifolds with General-Purpose IK Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10905)
*Thomas Cohn,Seiji Shaw,Harel Biggie,Travis Manderson,Nicholas Roy,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用逆函数定理从正向运动学雅可比计算解析IK参数化的梯度，并加入最小二乘域延拓与可达性约束的可优化描述，实现带约束的机器人轨迹优化，含真机演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带运动学等式约束的机械臂轨迹规划将可行运动限制在配置空间的零测度子流形上，需特殊算法处理。用解析IK参数化可行集是有效思路，但绝大多数IK函数由IKFast等元求解器自动生成，难以改写为可微形式，无法直接用于基于梯度的轨迹优化。

Method: 利用逆函数定理，从普通正向运动学雅可比中恢复解析IK参数化所需的梯度，从而无需修改IKFast等生成的IK函数；进一步提出最小二乘域延拓与便于优化的可达性约束描述，在可达工作空间之外仍保留梯度信号。

Result: 通过数值实验与下游任务验证方法有效性，并完成RB-Y1抓取盒子并放置到桌面的硬件演示。

Conclusion: 该梯度恢复方案使不可微的自动生成解析IK可用于梯度型轨迹优化，配合域延拓与可达性约束处理，能在实际硬件任务中稳定工作。

Abstract: Planning trajectories for robot manipulators under kinematic equality constraints restricts feasible motions to a measure-zero submanifold of the configuration space, requiring special algorithmic treatment. A promising strategy is parametrizing the set of feasible configurations using analytic inverse kinematics (IK). Bespoke analytic IK functions can be written to be differentiable, a necessary property for gradient-based trajectory optimization. But the vast majority of IK functions are computed by automated meta-solvers like IKFast, and are difficult to modify for differentiability. We present a new approach for computing gradients of analytic IK parameterizations: we leverage the inverse function theorem to recover the desired gradients from the ordinary forward kinematic Jacobian. Furthermore, we present a least-squares domain extension and an optimization-amenable description of the reachability constraint, which preserves gradient signal outside the reachable workspace. We demonstrate the efficacy of our approach through numerical experiments and downstream tasks, including a hardware demonstration of an RB-Y1 picking up a box and placing it on a table. Project website: https://cohnt.github.io/inverse-function-theorem-parameterization/

</details>


### [82] [IMLE-VLA: Fast Single-Step Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10915)
*Kian Hosseinkhani,Qinhe Peng,George Shramko,Mehran Aghabozorgi,Jianing Qian,Tristan Engst,Alireza Moazeni,Dinesh Jayaraman,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMLE-VLA 用单步条件生成器（cIMLE）替换扩散/流匹配的迭代动作头，消除多步采样瓶颈，在 π0.5 上把推理频率提升 3.67 倍并提高任务成功率与运动平滑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略（如 π0.5）将预训练视觉-语言骨干与连续动作头结合，动作头依赖扩散或流匹配的迭代多步采样（如 10 步 Euler），造成推理瓶颈，导致机器人运动出现"走走停停"、任务完成变慢。作者希望在不牺牲多模态动作覆盖与泛化能力的前提下，彻底去掉多步采样。

Method: 提出 IMLE-VLA：用单步条件生成器替代迭代动作头，通过条件隐式最大似然估计（cIMLE）目标训练。cIMLE 显式促进多模态动作分布的覆盖，避免朴素回归头的模式坍缩，同时无需任何多步采样。该方法作为动作头即插即用地应用于 π0.5。

Result: 推理频率提升 3.67 倍（55 Hz vs. 15 Hz），动作吞吐量最高提升 11 倍；LIBERO 40 任务上平均成功率 98.0%，为所有基线中最高且推理频率领先；在 LIBERO-plus 测试时扰动下保持 π0.5 的鲁棒性，而其他基线显著退化；Franka Emika Panda 四任务真机实验中运动更平滑（jerk 降低 2.2–3.0 倍）、任务完成更快，每个 episode 平均 VLA 推理时间减少 3.9–6.6 倍。

Conclusion: 单步 cIMLE 动作头可在保持甚至提升泛化与鲁棒性的同时，消除迭代采样瓶颈，显著提高 VLA 推理频率、动作吞吐与真机运动质量，说明"生成质量必须依赖多步采样"这一假设并非必要。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies leverage pretrained vision-language backbones to achieve strong cross-task generalization. A leading design couples this backbone with a dedicated continuous action head trained via diffusion or flow matching. However, such heads rely on iterative multi-step sampling, for example 10 Euler steps in $π_{0.5}$. This creates an inference bottleneck that produces stop-and-go movement in the robot and slower task completion. We introduce IMLE-VLA, which replaces the iterative action head with a single-step conditional generator trained via conditional Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (cIMLE). The cIMLE objective promotes multimodal action coverage, avoiding the mode collapse of naive regression heads while eliminating multi-step sampling entirely. When IMLE-VLA is applied to $π_{0.5}$, it increases inference frequency 3.67x (55 Hz vs. 15 Hz), enabling up to 11x higher action throughput. On the 40-task LIBERO benchmark, IMLE-VLA achieves the highest average success rate (98.0%) among all baselines while leading in inference frequency. Under the test-time perturbations of LIBERO-plus, IMLE-VLA retains $π_{0.5}$'s robustness while other baselines degrade sharply, confirming that the cIMLE head preserves generalization. Real-world experiments on a Franka Emika Panda across four tasks demonstrate smoother motion (2.2x to 3.0x lower jerk) and faster task completion, with IMLE-VLA outperforming $π_{0.5}$ on every task and reducing average VLA inference time per episode by 3.9x to 6.6x. Videos and code are available at https://kianhk6.github.io/IMLE-VLA/

</details>


### [83] [ObstaDiff: Generalizable Diffusion Policy Learning via Obstacle-aware Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10918)
*Jiawen Wang,Kevin Yao,Khalid Jawed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ObstaDiff：分解式扩散策略框架，配轻量障碍感知视觉编码器，将场景显式解耦为目标-障碍-背景表示，引导下游对齐策略在杂乱场景中生成避障末端轨迹，并在真实温室机器人试验中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人模仿学习多假设干净背景，缺乏显式避障运动生成机制，难以泛化到含非结构化障碍的杂乱真实场景（如农业温室），障碍物干扰下的策略鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出 ObstaDiff 分解式扩散策略：用轻量障碍感知视觉编码器提取结构化的目标-障碍-背景三层表征，驱动下游对齐策略生成朝向以目标为中心的瓶颈位姿（bottleneck pose）的末端轨迹，并在生成过程中显式推理周围障碍物。

Result: 在真实机器人温室场景中每方法 61 次试验、共 366 次执行：平均任务成功率 75.41%，平均障碍碰撞率仅 8.20%，优于代表性模仿学习基线，杂乱农业场景泛化能力提升。

Conclusion: 显式场景分解与障碍感知表征可有效提升扩散策略在杂乱真实环境中的避障与泛化能力，为农业等非结构化场景的机器人操作提供可行路径。

Abstract: Imitation learning has achieved impressive results in robotic manipulation, yet most existing approaches assume clean backgrounds and lack explicit mechanisms for obstacle-aware motion generation. Extending such policies to cluttered, real-world scenes with unstructured obstacles remains a key generalization challenge. We present ObstaDiff, a decomposed diffusion-policy framework with a lightweight obstacle-aware visual encoder. ObstaDiff extracts a structured target-obstacle-background representation, enabling the downstream alignment policy to generate end-effector trajectories toward a target-centered bottleneck pose while reasoning about surrounding obstacles. We evaluate ObstaDiff on 61 real-robot greenhouse trials per method (366 executions in total). ObstaDiff achieves 75.41% average task success and 8.20% average obstacle collision rate, outperforming representative imitation-learning baselines and improving generalization in cluttered agricultural scenes.

</details>


### [84] [Testing Between the Test Cases: Proving End-to-End Steering in Conditions You Never Drove](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10951)
*Menuka Ghalan,Charles Rodgers,Zachary D. Asher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将边界传播（bound propagation）形式化验证用于CARLA端到端转向网络，在无需重新仿真驾驶的情况下计算各扰动强度下转向漂移上界，发现仅靠仿真测试无法暴露的失效条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI自动驾驶测试的根本困境：模型通过全部测试条件仍可能在真实世界失效，仿真测试覆盖度有限且组合空间爆炸。作者希望用一个形式化方法直接回答"两次采样图像之间转向能漂移多少"这一仿真难以穷尽的问题。

Method: 在CARLA的仿真高速与城市主干道上训练两个小型端到端转向网络（各在清晰条件，以及清晰+雾+夜+低日照条件下训练，共四个模型），全部对照2.19 ft的车道偏离预算评估。采用bound propagation形式化方法读取训练权重，对捕获的两帧图像之间的扰动强度区间逐点计算转向漂移上界，单次计算即可覆盖133个位姿（若逐个枚举10种强度则需10^133种组合），在单GPU上数分钟完成。

Result: 形式化验证在未做仿真测试的情况下找到了使清晰条件训练策略失效的条件，并对测试用例之间的潜在失效给出了初步证据；单次计算在主干道上覆盖133个位姿（枚举需10^133组合），数分钟内在单GPU完成。

Conclusion: 形式化验证可作为仿真的可行补充，具备纳入自动驾驶验证与确认（V&V）流程的潜力；但其证据仅为CARLA仿真中的小型转向网络与初步结果，距离实车部署仍有差距。

Abstract: AI-based automated vehicle testing is challenging because a model that passes every test condition can still fail in the real world. Formal verification offers a way to directly address this gap. On a simulated highway and an arterial road we trained two small end-to-end steering networks each in CARLA, one on clear conditions alone and one on clear, fog, night and low sun. All four models were driven against a 2.19 ft lane-departure budget. Without driving again, we used bound propagation, a formal method that reads the trained weights, to compute how far steering can drift at every disturbance strength between two captured images. One calculation covers more than a campaign could drive: on the arterial it spans 133 poses, where ten intensities each would be 10^133 combinations, in minutes on one GPU. Not only did formal verification find conditions that broke the clear-trained policy without simulation testing, it provided some preliminary evidence for potential failures between the test cases. Our overall conclusion is that formal verification is a viable complement to simulation, and could be adopted as a part of verification and validation for automated driving.

</details>


### [85] [LTLDiff: Finite Linear Temporal Logic-Guided Data Generation and Diffusion Policies for Multi-agent Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11043)
*Chuhan Meng,Haiyan Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LTLDiff 将有限线性时序逻辑（LTLf）规范学习与扩散策略结合，用大语言模型从自然语言指令中学习任务 LTLf 公式，并以抽象语法树嵌入作为扩散策略训练与数据收集的条件，从而提升多智能体操作任务的协调性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体机器人操作需要满足任务级时序、逻辑与安全约束，而现有扩散策略在需要同时或顺序交互的任务中会出现失同步、动作顺序错误与协调失败等问题。

Method: 提出 LTLDiff 框架：(1) 用大语言模型从自然语言指令中学习每个任务对应的 LTLf 公式；(2) 采用抽象语法树表示获得定维逻辑向量嵌入；(3) 将该嵌入作为条件，用于逻辑引导的数据采集与基于扩散的策略训练，促使轨迹符合期望的时序与协调要求。

Result: 在多智能体 LTLf 操作任务上，与基线方法相比任务成功率取得提升。

Conclusion: 将时序逻辑规范学习与扩散策略条件化相结合，可有效实现协调的多智能体操作，验证了 LTLDiff 的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent robotic manipulation tasks require coordination among agents to satisfy task-level temporal, logical, and safety constraints. Recently, diffusion policies have been used to perform the task. However, they still suffer from desynchronization, incorrect action ordering, and coordination failures in tasks that require simultaneous or sequential multi-agent interaction. Therefore, LTLDiff is proposed as a framework that combines Finite Linear Temporal Logic (LTLf) specification learning for both the generation of demonstrations and learning via diffusion policies. Each task has a specific LTLf formula that is learned from a set of natural language instructions using a large-scale language model. To enable a fixed-dimensional vector embedding of the learned specification from the language model, LTLf uses an abstract syntax tree representation scheme. This embedding of logic serves as a condition for (i) logic-guided data collection and (ii) diffusion-based policy training, encouraging trajectories that are consistent with the desired ordering and coordination requirements. Experiments on multi-agent LTLDiff manipulation tasks demonstrate improved task success rates compared to the baseline. Together, these contributions demonstrate the effectiveness of LTLDiff for coordinated multi-agent manipulation.

</details>


### [86] [Gait-Dependent Effects on Quadruped Locomotion for Load-Carrying using Passive Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11059)
*Giovanni B. Dessy,Claudio Semini,Victor Barasuol*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文在仿真中分析被动机械臂的刚度-阻尼配置（过阻尼 vs 欠阻尼）如何影响四足机器人负重行走，结果表明欠阻尼阻抗在爬行步态下会增大被动关节振荡并降低ZMP稳定裕度，并以步态-负载-刚度-阻尼映射图总结结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动机械接口比主动机械臂更轻量，适合四足机器人负载运输，但其阻抗会将负载动力学与运动模式直接耦合，因此需要弄清被动臂刚度-阻尼参数如何在步态与负载变化下影响运动稳定性。

Method: 纯仿真研究：在平地行走条件下比较过阻尼与欠阻尼被动臂阻抗配置；以爬行步态（支撑多边形明确）为主要对象评估ZMP裕度，小跑步态仅作为被动臂动态激励案例，不用于ZMP稳定性直接对比；结果汇总为步态-负载-刚度-阻尼映射图。

Result: 爬行步态下，欠阻尼阻抗会加剧被动关节振荡，并可能降低相对于支撑多边形的ZMP裕度；映射图给出了不同步态-负载组合下ZMP裕度衰减的量化分布。

Conclusion: 被动臂阻抗选择与步态、负载共同决定负重行走稳定性，爬行步态宜采用偏阻尼配置以抑制振荡并保持ZMP裕度；小跑仅可作为动态激励验证场景。该结论限于仿真与平地条件，尚需实物验证。

Abstract: Passive mechanical interfaces offer a lightweight alternative to actuated manipulators for quadruped payload carrying, but their impedance directly couples the payload dynamics with the locomotion pattern. This paper analyzes how passive-arm stiffness-damping selection affects payload-carrying locomotion under different gait and payload conditions. We compare damped and underdamped passive-arm impedance configurations in simulation during flat-ground locomotion. For crawl gaits, where the support polygon remains well defined, the results show that underdamped impedance increases passive-joint oscillations and can reduce the ZMP margin with respect to the support polygon. Trot is retained as a dynamic excitation case for the passive arm, but it is not used for direct ZMP-margin stability comparison. The results are summarized in gait-payload-stiffness-damping maps, where ZMP-margin reduction is evaluated for crawl gaits and trot is retained only as a passive-arm excitation case.

</details>


### [87] [Freehand Sketching for End-User Programming of Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11078)
*Riwa Karam,Ian Kuo,Magnus Egerstedt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究以徒手草图作为机器人集群几何构型的终端用户编程接口，用户绘图表达空间意图，集群自主提取目标队形点、构建刚性队形图、分配机器人节点并执行抗镜像的分布式编队控制；20名用户生成42个图形，SUS均分为84.25，表明徒手草图是高可用性的自然人-集群交互抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人集群应用日益需要非专家用户可用的交互方式，而传统的集群编队编程需要机器人学与编程专业知识，因此希望找到一种直观的终端用户编程抽象来指定集群几何构型。

Method: 提出sketch-to-swarm框架：用户徒手绘制图形传达空间意图，系统自动提取目标编队点、构建刚性编队图、进行机器人-编队节点分配，并执行分布式编队控制，且带有对非预期镜像构型的防护机制；通过20人、42个几何图形的人因实验评估可用性，采用SUS量表。

Result: 20名参与者共生成42个几何形状，接口SUS平均得分84.25，按惯例属高感知可用性水平，支持徒手草图作为直观的人-集群协作交互抽象。

Conclusion: 徒手草图可让不具备机器人学或编程经验的用户直观指定机器人集群构型，是人-集群协作中可行的交互抽象；但本文主要贡献为交互与可用性评估，未提供大规模真机部署或控制性能对比证据。

Abstract: Robot swarms are increasingly used in applications where accessible interaction with non-expert users is desirable. This paper investigates freehand sketching as an end-user programming interface for specifying robot swarm geometries. Users communicate spatial intent through a drawing, while the swarm autonomously extracts target formation points, constructs a rigid formation graph, assigns robots to formation nodes, and executes distributed formation control with a guarantee against unintended reflected formations. The resulting sketch-to-swarm framework is evaluated through a human study examining the usability of freehand formation specification. Twenty participants generated $42$ geometric shapes, and the interface achieved a mean System Usability Scale score of $84.25$, which conventionally indicates high perceived usability. The results support freehand sketching as an intuitive interaction abstraction for human-swarm collaboration without requiring robotics or programming expertise.

</details>


### [88] [RIDE: Relocalization-Informed Depth Estimation with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11079)
*Jiarong Lian,Zhe Xiao,Zhaoyang Zhang,Wei Li,Ruizhi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RIDE利用PnP-RANSAC内点对应提供的稀疏度量深度，结合预训练视频深度模型先验，在度量尺度3DGS地图下实现机器人RGB流的稠密度量深度估计，并通过全局/局部校正与时序记忆处理欠观测与间断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重定位（render-match-PnP）流程已建立像素与3D地图点的对应关系，其中蕴含度量几何信息，但通常只用于位姿恢复，其支撑稠密深度估计的潜力被忽视。

Method: 以度量尺度的3DGS模型为地图，从PnP-RANSAC内点对应导出稀疏度量深度观测，与预训练视频深度模型的几何先验融合；引入全局与局部深度校正模块及时序记忆机制，以应对观测分布不均与间歇性，从而在度量尺度初始化后跨越短观测空窗期持续输出稠密深度。在公开RGB-D视频上训练，在机器人序列上零微调评估。

Result: 在机器人序列上零微调评估，相比仅做尺度标定的基线，深度精度与时序一致性均有提升；验证了定位几何既可支持位姿恢复也可支持稠密机器人感知。

Conclusion: 将定位中的稀疏度量对应关系转化为稠密度量深度的监督/先验信号是可行的，RIDE展示了定位几何在机器人稠密感知中的复用价值，为深度估计与重定位的联合利用提供了思路。

Abstract: Render--match--PnP relocalization establishes correspondences between query image pixels and 3D map points for camera pose recovery, but their potential to support dense depth estimation is often overlooked. To exploit this geometric information, we present RIDE, which estimates dense metric depth from a robot's RGB stream. Given a metrically scaled 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model, RIDE combines sparse metric depth observations derived from PnP-RANSAC inlier correspondences with the geometric prior of a pretrained video-depth model. To handle uneven and intermittent observations, it integrates global and local depth correction with temporal memory, supporting depth estimation through short observation gaps after metric scale initialization. Trained on public RGB-D videos, RIDE is evaluated on robot sequences without fine tuning. Experiments show improved depth accuracy and temporal consistency over scale-only calibration, demonstrating how localization geometry can support both pose recovery and dense robot perception.

</details>


### [89] [Harness Robotic OS: A Unified Embodied-Agent Runtime for Closed-Loop Quadruped Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11225)
*Yaoyuan Yan,Zhiyou Heng,Haoxiang Jie,Gang Liu,Hongjie Yan,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一的具身智能体运行时 HROS 及其住宅社区巡检实例 Argos，整合四足机器人导航、多模态感知、语音交互与记忆机制，并引入安全门控的自演进闭环，实现可部署的闭环巡检系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足巡检系统以任务专用接口拼装各功能模块，导致上下文协调、知识复用与受控自适应困难，缺乏统一、可追溯的具身运行时架构。

Method: HROS 划分为机器人运行时、具身自主技能、认知智能体运行时、交互与运维四个平面；共享上下文连接物理状态与智能体推理，流式 ASR/TTS 支持语音任务交互，分层工作/情景/语义记忆保存运行知识，安全门控自演进循环将执行轨迹转为带版本的候选更新。Argos 集成 Vbot 四足平台、Fast-LIO2 定位建图、Hobot-Stereo 深度感知、PCT-Planner 全局规划、EGO-Planner 局部运动生成、OpenClaw 编排 Qwen3-VL 巡检分析。

Result: 住宅场景实验中航点可达率 100%，室外定位误差低于 10 cm，局部避障响应延迟低于 200 ms，代表性隐患检测率 85–95%，告警投递与结构化报告生成成功率 99%。

Conclusion: 验证了导航与巡检闭环的可部署性；HROS 为具备记忆增强、语音感知与持续可改进能力的具身巡检智能体提供了可扩展软件基础。

Abstract: Autonomous property inspection requires more than robust robot navigation: a deployable system must connect heterogeneous sensing, reusable autonomy capabilities, multimodal scene understanding, human interaction, and enterprise response within a traceable operational loop. Existing quadruped inspection systems commonly integrate these functions through task-specific interfaces, making contextual coordination, knowledge reuse, and controlled adaptation difficult. This paper presents \textit{Harness Robotic OS} (HROS), a unified embodied-agent runtime, and Argos, its realization for residential-community inspection. HROS organizes the system into robot runtime, embodied autonomy skills, cognitive agent runtime, and interaction and operations planes. A shared context connects physical state with agent reasoning; streaming ASR/TTS supports voice-based mission interaction; hierarchical working, episodic, and semantic memory preserves operational knowledge; and a safety-gated self-evolution loop converts execution traces into versioned candidate updates without permitting unconstrained online modification. The Argos prototype integrates a Vbot quadruped, Fast-LIO2 localization and mapping, Hobot-Stereo depth perception, PCT-Planner global planning, EGO-Planner local motion generation, and OpenClaw-orchestrated Qwen3-VL inspection analysis. Experiments in a residential property environment achieved 100\% waypoint reachability, outdoor localization error below 10~cm, local obstacle-response latency below 200~ms, representative hazard-detection rates of 85--95\%, and 99\% success in alarm delivery and structured-report generation. These results validate the deployed navigation and inspection closed loop, while HROS provides an extensible software foundation for memory-augmented, voice-aware, and continuously improvable embodied inspection agents.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Noise Steering: Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning for Generative Robot Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11270)
*Pengfei Zhang,Teng Sun,Xianchao Xiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DLSRL框架，在冻结的生成式机器人策略中同时操控初始噪声潜变量与中间动作表示潜变量，通过残差适配器注入实现表示级控制，加速在线策略适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练生成式机器人策略虽具备表达性动作先验，但强化学习通常只调节初始噪声空间，无法调控生成过程中间动作表示，导致性能退化与低效。

Method: DLSRL：Actor网络输出两类潜变量——引导行为生成的初始噪声潜变量，以及用于中间特征调制的动作表示潜变量；后者映射为适配器特征，经残差连接注入中间动作token隐藏状态；双通道控制无需更新基座策略。

Result: 在多种生成式策略架构与机器人操作任务上，DLSRL有效加速在线策略适配并取得有竞争力的性能；代码开源。

Conclusion: 在冻结生成器内部实现表示级控制是对噪声引导的有效补充，为生成式策略的高效在线强化学习微调提供新范式。

Abstract: Pretrained generative robot policies learn expressive action priors from demonstrations. However, existing reinforcement learning methods only steer the noisy space but fail to modulate intermediate action representations during the generation process, resulting in performance degradation and inefficiency. To address this limitation, we propose a novel Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning (DLSRL) framework, which complements initial-noise steering with representation-level control inside the frozen generator. Specifically, our actor network predicts two distinct latent variables: an initial-noise latent variable that steers behavior generation, and an action-representation latent variable for intermediate feature modulation. Moreover, this representation latent variable is mapped to adapter features and ingeniously injected into the hidden states of intermediate action tokens via residual connections. Our dual-control design enables direct adjustment of action representations without updating the base policy. Experiments across generative policy architectures and robotic manipulation tasks show that DLSRL effectively accelerates online robot policy adaptation and achieves competitive performance. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}.

</details>


### [91] [2AM: Grounding Agent-Side Memory as Guidance for Steerable Action Models in Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11308)
*Yutong Hu,Fengjiao Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Renaud Detry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 2AM 将任务记忆完全放入多模态 Agent，用单一只靠 RGB、回合内无状态的 Action Model 执行动作，通过自然语言子任务与 2D 抓取/放置/移动提示实现可操控的 VLA，在 LIBERO-Mem 上把平均完成率从最强基线 14.8% 提升到 76.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要记忆，但现有 agentic 系统（VLA+规划器+几何工具、额外深度/标定）混淆了增益归因：收益可能来自更丰富观测或替代运动工具，失败可能源自策略或欠规格的语言接口。作者希望隔离出"记忆位置"与"Agent 对动作模型的操控精度"这两个变量。

Method: 刻意约束设计：减少工具广度但增加接口带宽。多模态 Agent 是任务记忆的唯一持有者，单一 RGB 输入、回合间无状态的 Action Model 是动作的唯一执行者。Agent 将交互历史编译为子任务语言，以及可选的 2D 抓取、放置、移动提示，在不同时间尺度上绑定物理意图。为让 VLA 学会这种可操控性，在演示中增强结构化提示标签，并在条件 dropout、空间噪声、时间抖动下训练，以容忍 Agent 输出的不完美。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Mem 上，不使用深度、在线几何或基于规划器的物体运动，2AM 达到 76.3% 平均完成率，比最强已报告基线 14.8% 高出 61.5 个百分点；另有 63.0% 宽松成功率和 11.8% 严格成功率。

Conclusion: 任务记忆可以保留在 Agent 侧而非动作策略内部；动作模型的能力不仅取决于策略学到了什么，还取决于 Agent 能以多高的精度操控它。接口带宽是长时程操作中被低估的关键变量。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires memory, but not necessarily inside the action policy. To address such tasks, current agentic systems often combine VLAs with planners and geometric tools, sometimes using additional depth or calibrated geometry. These systems confound attribution: gains may come from richer observations or alternative motor tools, while failures may stem from either the policy or an under-specified language interface. We isolate this question through a deliberately constrained design: less tool breadth, but greater interface bandwidth. 2AM makes a multimodal Agent the sole holder of task memory and a single RGB-based, episodically stateless Action Model the sole executor of task-relevant motion. The Agent compiles interaction history into subtask language and optional 2D grasp, place, and move hints that bind its physical intention at different time scales. To teach this steerability to the VLA, we augment demonstrations with structured hint labels and train under condition dropout, spatial noise, and temporal jitter to tolerate imperfect Agent outputs. On LIBERO-Mem, without depth, online geometry, or planner-based object motion, 2AM reaches 76.3% average completion, a 61.5-point improvement over the strongest reported baseline of 14.8%, together with 63.0% relaxed and 11.8% strict success. These results show that task memory can remain Agent-side. They further show that Action Model capability depends not only on what the policy has learned, but on how precisely the Agent can steer it.

</details>


### [92] [Modular Kinematic Reduction of Closed-Chain Mechanisms Using Path Assembly and Defect Homotopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11338)
*Mohammad Dastranj,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PACDM框架，用路径装配的闭环微分映射统一处理闭运动链的约束消解与运动学降维，在7自由度重载机械臂上验证，精度达1e-10 rad量级，并加速45.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭运动链中主动与被动机通过非线性闭环约束耦合，导致模块化建模困难：闭环约束求解繁琐、被动坐标恢复缺乏统一有效手段，且逐样本求解代价高，缺乏模块化、可组合且高效的闭环处理与降维方法。

Method: 核心是Path-Assembled Closure Differential Mapping。每个闭环单元比较具有公共端点的两条有序变换路径，用SE(3)对数映射表达路径失配，并由局部变换导数装配相应雅可比；由最少成对闭环单元构造多路径模块，用秩揭示分析筛选局部独立的标量约束；用缺陷同伦沿可行且正则的连续路径从近似估计恢复满足闭环的被动机坐标；在正则位形下用隐式微分得到局部主动到被动的微分映射，再嵌入预测-校正连续化流程实现给定运动求解。

Result: 在含双路径与三路径闭链模块的7自由度重载机械臂上评估：与Simscape Multibody对比，轨迹均方根误差低于8.5×10^-10 rad；预测-校正连续化相较在每个轨迹采样点都执行缺陷同伦约快45.8倍。

Conclusion: PACDM以路径装配方式把闭环约束的表达、排序、被动机恢复与主动-被动微分映射统一为模块化框架，兼容多路径闭链，兼顾数值精度与计算效率，适用于重载闭链机械臂等复杂机构的运动学建模与降维。

Abstract: Closed kinematic chains complicate modular modeling by coupling active and passive coordinates through nonlinear closure constraints. This paper presents a Path-Assembled Closure Differential Mapping (PACDM) framework for modular closure resolution and kinematic reduction. Each closure element compares two ordered transformation paths with common endpoints, with their mismatch expressed through the logarithm on SE(3) and the corresponding Jacobian assembled from local transformation derivatives. Multi-path modules are constructed from a minimal set of pairwise closure elements, while rank-revealing analysis selects locally independent scalar constraints. A defect homotopy recovers closure-consistent passive coordinates from approximate estimates along a feasible and regular continuation path. At regular configurations, implicit differentiation yields the local active-to-passive differential mapping, which is subsequently used in a predictor-corrector continuation procedure for prescribed motion. The framework is evaluated on a seven-degree-of-freedom heavy-duty manipulator containing two-path and three-path closed-chain modules. Comparison with Simscape Multibody yields trajectory root-mean-square errors below 8.5 x 10^-10 rad, while predictor-corrector continuation is approximately 45.8 times faster than applying defect homotopy at every trajectory sample.

</details>


### [93] [Morphology-Aware Human Motion Retargeting for Wheeled-Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11357)
*Chenbo Xia,Chao Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套可复现的流水线，将多数据集SMPLX人体动作重定向为Galaxea R1 Pro轮式人形机器人的可执行移动-操作行为，并通过Isaac Lab训练21维BaseDecode策略实现物理可跟踪的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-人形重定向多集中于足式平台，轮式人形系统在耦合移动与操作方面研究较少；且轮式人形无腿关节，需将人体下肢运动重新分配至底盘运动与躯干姿态，同时保留操作相关的臂部几何。

Method: 结合GMR可配置通用运动重定向与BeyondMimic物理仿真学习框架；采用规范化体型预处理、平面底盘归一化、形态感知微分逆运动学、以肩为根的层级式手臂重定向，以及用于弯腰/下蹲的连续躯干替代；参考twist驱动的规划层在迟滞、运动学连续性、加速度与执行器速率限制下将平面底盘运动解码为三轮转向与滚动指令；在Isaac Lab训练21维BaseDecode策略，含方向性关节限位缩放、聚焦上身跟踪与分阶段轮地接触奖励。

Result: 构建了从人体动作数据到物理可跟踪的轮式人形移动-操作完整桥接系统，而非仅可视化的重定向器；定量策略对比结果待后续修订补充。

Conclusion: 该工作为轮式人形提供了可复现的动作重定向到物理仿真执行流水线，但缺少定量评估与真机验证。

Abstract: Human-to-humanoid retargeting has largely been studied on legged platforms, while comparatively few wheeled-humanoid systems support coupled locomotion and manipulation from general human motion. Building on GMR's configurable general-motion retargeting and BeyondMimic's physically simulated R1 Pro learning framework, we present a reproducible pipeline that converts multi-dataset SMPLX motion into executable loco-manipulation behavior for the Galaxea R1 Pro wheeled humanoid. The robot has a planar three-wheel base, a serial torso, and two arms but no leg joints, so human lower-body motion must be redistributed across base motion and torso posture without sacrificing manipulation-relevant arm geometry. Our pipeline combines canonical body-shape preprocessing, planar-base normalization, morphology-aware differential inverse kinematics, shoulder-rooted hierarchical arm retargeting, and continuous torso substitution for bending and squatting. A reference-twist-driven planning layer then decodes planar base motion into continuous three-wheel steering and rolling commands subject to hysteresis, kinematic continuity, acceleration, and actuator-rate limits. Finally, a 21-dimensional BaseDecode policy is trained in Isaac Lab with directional joint-limit scaling, focused upper-body tracking, and a staged wheel-contact reward. The resulting system provides a complete bridge from human motion data to physically trackable wheeled-humanoid loco-manipulation rather than a visualization-only retargeter; quantitative policy comparisons remain scheduled for a later revision.

</details>


### [94] [GeoTrussRover: Morphological Computation with Contact-Semantic Control Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11361)
*Muyuan Ma,Yi Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuanyan Zheng,Ruiqi Hu,Boxuan Ke,Zhenyu Chen,Yicong Lin,Xin Hao Yang,Daliang Xiao,Zhinan Hou,Wanhao Niu,Yuan Sun,Yan Yang,Yue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GeoTrussRover：电动变几何桁架+轮式底盘，通过接触语义基元与物理约束投影实现低维协调复用，显著减少重算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定形体机器人无法跨越障碍时需改变接触几何；变几何桁架(VGT)可将形变分布到承载结构，但与移动底盘耦合后形成高维协调难题，需要一种低维、可复用的形态-运动协调表示来降低规划与控制成本。

Method: 构建电驱动VGT+轮式底盘平台；求解一次源穿越任务，抽取四个接触语义基元，描述21个构件的协调关系；用物理约束投影将基元适配到未见台阶高度(保持相同接触拓扑)；相位可行时复用原运动，某相位违反新物理约束时仅重算该相位；再用全空间QP跟踪适配运动并修正构件与轮子误差。

Result: 0.10m→0.075m迁移时目标函数评估次数比完全重算减少63.7%；接触相位可行性分析覆盖0.10–0.46m台阶(1.08–4.97轮半径)，上界接近理论可行边界；电动原型实机穿越2.11轮半径。

Conclusion: 低维接触语义表示可将任务协调存储于超冗余承载形态中，并在运动过程中复用，为变几何移动机器人的形态-运动协调提供了可迁移、可局部修正的框架。

Abstract: Reconfigurable robots can change their contact geometry when a fixed body cannot negotiate an obstacle. A variable-geometry truss (VGT) distributes this shape change through a load-bearing structure, but coupling it to a mobile base creates a high-dimensional coordination problem. GeoTrussRover combines an electrically actuated VGT, a wheeled base, and contact-semantic morphology planning and control. We solve one source traversal and extract four contact-semantic primitives that describe coordination among 21 members. Physics-constrained projection adapts them to unseen step heights with the same contact topology. When every phase remains feasible, adaptation does not recompute the complete motion. If one phase violates the new physical constraints, only that phase is recomputed. A full-space QP then tracks the adapted motion and corrects member and wheel errors. For transfer from 0.10m to 0.075m, the method reduces objective-function evaluations by 63.7% relative to full recomputation. Contact-phase feasibility analysis covers step heights from 0.10 to 0.46m, or 1.08 to 4.97 wheel radii, with the upper value near the theoretical feasible boundary. The electric prototype traverses 2.11 wheel radii. The resulting low-dimensional representation stores task coordination in a hyper-redundant, load-bearing morphology and reuses it during locomotion.

</details>


### [95] [SwarmNxt: Open-source Software-Hardware Platform for Fast and Agile Aerial Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11382)
*Charbel Toumieh,Niel Mistry,Benjamin Jarvis,Simon Jeger,Peize Liu,Shaojie Shen,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwarmNxt：基于开源OmniNxt硬件的开源无人机集群平台，提供硬件组装教程、并行部署自动化与ROS 2自主导航框架，并用6机去中心化避障规划与4机机载深度估计集群飞行两类实机实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人集群对应急救援、安防等时间敏感任务有巨大潜力，但鲁棒的自主集群导航仍是难题。现有商用无人机多为闭源、机载算力不足，难以支持敏捷的视觉集群飞行；多机软件部署与维护工程量大。因此需要一个开放、可复现的物理集群研究基础设施。

Method: 构建开源软件平台SwarmNxt，依托开源OmniNxt无人机硬件：包含详细硬件装配说明与视频教程、并行软件部署与集群级更新自动化工具，以及基于ROS 2的自主导航框架，将先进的控制、规划与深度估计集成到单一ROS 2多智能体系统。

Result: 两项实机实验：6机集群进行去中心化规划并实现高速机间避碰；4机集群在布满障碍的环境中执行带机载深度估计的集群飞行。两实验均在室内进行，全局位置取自外部动捕，感知、规划与控制均机载运行。

Conclusion: SwarmNxt为物理集群实验提供了开放研究基础设施，验证了多机去中心化规划与机载深度估计集群飞行的可行性，降低集群研发与部署门槛。

Abstract: Aerial robot swarms have the potential to transform time-critical safety, security, and search-and-rescue operations. By coordinating multiple robots, they can rapidly survey disaster sites, map collapsed or GPS-denied environments, and search cluttered areas faster than a single robot, reducing response times and minimizing risks to first responders. Realizing this potential, however, requires robust autonomous swarm navigation, which remains an active research challenge. Progress is further constrained by existing platforms, as commercial drones are often closed-source or lack the onboard computational resources needed for agile, vision-based collective flight. Moreover, developing, deploying, and maintaining software across multiple aerial robots requires significant engineering effort. To address these challenges, we present SwarmNxt, an open-source software platform built on the open-source OmniNxt drone hardware. SwarmNxt provides an end-to-end toolkit, including detailed hardware assembly instructions with a video tutorial, automation tools for parallel software deployment and swarm-wide updates, and a ROS 2-based framework for autonomous navigation. The platform integrates state-of-the-art control, planning, and depth estimation into a single ROS 2 multi-agent system, providing an open research infrastructure for physical swarm experimentation. We validate SwarmNxt through two real-world experiments: a six-drone swarm performing decentralized planning with high-speed inter-drone collision avoidance, and a four-drone swarm executing collective flight with onboard depth estimation in an obstacle-filled environment. Both experiments were run indoors with global position from external motion capture; perception, planning, and control run onboard.

</details>


### [96] [Safety-aware Skill Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11433)
*A K M Nadimul Haque,Sheila Sutjipto,Marc G. Carmichael,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Dist-GPRL：以高斯过程对技能参数化，顺序自适应稀疏轨迹 via-points 的局部窗口，结合 HAP 安全子空间先验与动态距离场奖励，实现动态环境下的安全技能适配，并完成仿真到真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习的技能适配通常依赖固定观测或严格控制的探索等限制性假设以维持稳定；在杂乱动态环境中，不受限探索易导致不安全行为与学习不稳定，尤其当任务相关观测靠近障碍或涉及运动物体时。

Method: 在 GP 技能参数化基础上，顺序自适应稀疏轨迹 via-points 的重叠局部窗口，而非每步修改整个技能；策略输出经由 GP 协方差结构关联，产生时间一致的轨迹更新并降低动作空间与信用分配难度。安全引导包含两部分：由 Hausdorff Approximation Planner 导出的安全子空间先验，将探索偏向可行区域；动态更新的距离场间隙与梯度奖励提供局部障碍感知。另加轨迹运动学相似性正则项，保持示教的速度与加速度特性。

Result: 在两个动态物体操作任务的仿真中评估，并将学习策略迁移至真实机器人执行；相较基线，任务成功率更高、碰撞频率更低、学习更稳定，同时保持示教技能的运动学特征。

Conclusion: 距离感知与安全引导的 GP 技能适配框架可在动态杂乱环境中稳定学习安全轨迹，并具备仿真到真机的可迁移性。

Abstract: Skill adaptation frameworks based on reinforcement learning often require restrictive assumptions to maintain stability, such as fixed observations or tightly controlled exploration schedules. In cluttered and dynamic environments, however, unrestricted exploration can lead to unsafe behaviour and unstable learning, particularly when task-relevant observations lie near obstacles or involve moving objects. In this work, we present Dist-GPRL, a distance-aware and safety-guided reinforcement learning framework for structured robot skill adaptation. Building upon Gaussian Process (GP)-based skill parameterisation, our framework sequentially adapts overlapping local windows of sparse trajectory via-points rather than modifying the complete skill at every policy step. Raw policy outputs are correlated through the GP covariance structure, producing temporally coherent trajectory updates while reducing the action-space and credit-assignment difficulties associated with global trajectory adaptation. Safety is incorporated through two complementary forms of guidance. A safe-subspace prior derived from the Hausdorff Approximation Planner (HAP) biases policy exploration toward feasible regions, while dynamically updated distance field clearance and gradient rewards provide local obstacle awareness. A trajectory-kinematics similarity regulariser further preserves the demonstrated velocity and acceleration characteristics during adaptation. We evaluate the framework on two dynamic object-manipulation tasks in simulation and transfer the learned policy to real-world robot execution. Experimental results demonstrate higher task success, lower collision frequency, and more stable learning than the baselines, while preserving the kinematic characteristics of the demonstrated skill.

</details>


### [97] [FARM: Reading Failure Signals from the Internal Predictive States of a Frozen Robotic World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11445)
*Haoran Pei,Mingrui Luo,Senbao Wang,Haoran Lv,Jie Guo,Sheng Zhong,Ruixi Ci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FARM 仅训练 33,985 参数的监督读出层，从冻结的 VLA-JEPA 世界模型预测状态中解码逐步失败分数与因果轨迹风险，实现可迁移、低开销的机器人执行监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人可靠部署需要在线失败监控，但现有监控器多依赖代理信号或需训练专用组件。作者追问：冻结预训练机器人世界模型的内部预测状态中，是否已蕴含可直接解码的失败信息。

Method: 在冻结的 VLA-JEPA 预测状态之上训练轻量监督读出层（约 3.4 万参数），输出逐步失败分数与因果轨迹风险；采用五折 out-of-fold 评估，覆盖 7 个源任务与 10 任务基准（对比 15 个匹配基线）；在 PIPER X、SO-101、Franka 四种真实机器人群体上测试固定读出迁移与仅读出层适配。

Result: 五折 OOF 在 7 个源任务上达到 85.68/88.59 的 pooled AUROC/AUPRC；10 任务基准 Seen 表现优于全部 15 个匹配基线。跨四种真实机器人群体，无需更新预测主干即可迁移。能区分失败与部分因果历史；冻结状态可用后平均 CUDA 延迟仅增加 0.2256 ms。

Conclusion: 冻结的预测式世界模型状态可作为可复用特征，支撑因果性、可迁移且低开销的执行监控；失败信息已内在编码于预测状态中，无需重训主干。

Abstract: Reliable robot deployment requires online failure monitoring, yet existing monitors mainly derive risk from proxy signals or train dedicated monitoring components. We ask whether the internal predictive states of a frozen pretrained robotic world model already contain directly decodable failure information. Failure-Aware Readout from World Models (FARM) trains only a 33,985-parameter supervised readout over frozen VLA-JEPA predictive states, producing step-wise failure scores and causal trajectory risk. Five-fold out-of-fold evaluation across seven source tasks reaches 85.68/88.59 pooled AUROC/AUPRC, and FARM gives the best Seen performance among 15 matched baselines on the 10-task benchmark. Across four real-robot populations on PIPER X, SO-101, and Franka, fixed-readout transfer and readout-only adaptation test deployment shifts without updating the predictive backbone. FARM also discriminates failures from partial causal histories and adds 0.2256 ms mean CUDA latency once the frozen state is available. These results support frozen predictive world-model states as reusable features for causal, transferable, and low-overhead execution monitoring.

</details>


### [98] [3D Euler-Angle Orientation Control for Two-Ray Fading Mitigation in Maritime Air-to-Sea Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11476)
*Mohammed Bajja,Abdoul Karim A. H. Saliah,Hajar El Hammouti,Daniel Bonilla Licea,Giuseppe Silano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将多旋翼全三维姿态作为物理层控制变量，通过天线相位中心位移调控海面双径干涉，使链路由深度衰落转为相长干涉，并用约束NMPC跟踪实现吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上空-海链路以视距直射波与海面反射波的叠加为主，破坏性叠加会产生深度衰落，严重降低通信质量。已有缓解方案多优化无人机位置或轨迹，而姿态这一自由度的潜力未被利用。

Method: 在小角度与远场假设下，将相长干涉条件化简为欧拉角上的仿射约束，得到闭式的最小范数姿态解族；用可微软最小值规则生成光滑参考姿态，并由约束非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）在全驱动倾转多旋翼上跟踪，同时满足执行器限幅。

Result: 相比仅俯仰（pitch-only）基准，累计吞吐量提升11.4%；相比零姿态基线提升22.2%，同时保持轨迹跟踪精度并遵守执行器约束。

Conclusion: 姿态可作为通信物理层的有效控制变量，在几乎不牺牲飞行性能的前提下显著缓解海上双径衰落，为通信-控制协同设计提供了闭式、可落地的方案。

Abstract: Maritime Air-to-Sea links are dominated by a line-of-sight ray and a sea-surface reflected ray whose destructive combination produces deep fades. Existing mitigation strategies optimize Unmanned Aerial Vehicle position or trajectory but leave attitude unexploited. This paper treats the full three dimensional attitude as a physical-layer control variable that shapes the two-ray interference through antenna phase-center displacement. Under small-angle and far-field assumptions, the constructive-interference condition reduces to an affine constraint in the Euler angles and admits a closed-form family of minimum-norm attitude candidates. A differentiable soft-minimum rule yields a smooth reference tracked by a constrained Nonlinear Model Predictive Control controller on a fully-actuated tilting multirotor. The proposed scheme increases cumulative throughput by 11.4% over a pitch-only benchmark and 22.2% over a zero-orientation baseline, while preserving trajectory tracking and respecting actuator limits.

</details>


### [99] [CARLAverse: A Highly Modular, Distributed, and Multimodal Framework for Human-in-the-Loop Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11478)
*Patrick Rebling,Philipp Nenninger,Reiner Kriesten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARLAverse：开源多模态HITL仿真生态，集成驾驶/骑行/步行模拟器，采用分布式物理架构将高频率触觉控制回路本地计算、NPC物理与全局交通交由中心CARLA服务器，实现跨机构可扩展人机在环实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需在混合交通场景中测试，涉及弱势道路使用者（VRU），而纯人工代理难以复现真实人类社交协商行为。human-in-the-loop仿真器可安全研究此类交互，但现有多智能体平台受网络延迟与同步约束限制，无法支撑高保真触觉反馈。

Method: 扩展模块化硬件抽象，将DrivoCARLA（驾驶）、CycloCARLA（骑行）、WalkoCARLA（步行）三类模拟器集成到共享虚拟环境。核心为分布式物理架构：延迟敏感的ego动力学与高频力反馈在客户端本地计算，中心CARLA服务器负责NPC物理与全局交通编排，从而解耦触觉控制回路与网络瓶颈。

Result: 实现无需牺牲物理沉浸感即可跨机构扩展的HITL实验平台；给出开源代码与文档链接，但摘要未提供定量性能评测（延迟、同步精度或用户实验数据）。

Conclusion: CARLAverse通过分布式物理与多模式VRU模拟器集成，为混合交通人机交互研究提供可扩展基础设施；其实证价值有待延迟指标与真实用户实验验证。

Abstract: The development of autonomous driving demands comprehensive testing in mixed-traffic scenarios involving vulnerable road users (VRUs), where purely artificial agents often fail to capture authentic human social negotiations. While human-in-the-loop (HITL) simulators enable safe investigation of these interactions, existing multi-agent platforms struggle with the network latency and synchronization constraints required for high-fidelity haptic feedback. To resolve this, we present CARLAverse, an open-source, multimodal simulation ecosystem. Extending modular hardware abstraction, CARLAverse integrates driving (DrivoCARLA), cycling (CycloCARLA), and pedestrian (WalkoCARLA) simulators into a shared virtual environment. Its core methodological contribution is a distributed physics architecture: latency-critical ego dynamics and high-frequency force feedback are computed locally on client nodes, while a central CARLA server orchestrates non-player character (NPC) physics and global traffic. By decoupling haptic control loops from network bottlenecks, CARLAverse enables scalable, cross-institutional HITL experiments without compromising physical immersion. Code and documentation: https://git.ieem-ka.de/simulator-environments/carlaverse

</details>


### [100] [Using Automated Vehicles Operational Data to Confirm Safety and Anticipate Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11549)
*Riccardo Donà,Espedito Rusciano,Germana Trentadue,Anastasios Tsakalidis,Maria Cristina Galassi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 分析欧盟自动化驾驶系统（ADS）在用监测与报告（ISMR）数据采集法规框架，借鉴UNECE最佳实践及核能、交通领域的数据采集经验，以支撑ADS安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统型式认证（homologation）验证方案无法覆盖ADS在真实运行中可能出现的大量未知场景，需借助真实世界数据采集来确认安全目标。

Method: 政策/框架分析：梳理欧盟新法规中的数据采集条款，溯源至UNECE ISMR最佳实践及核能生产、交通运输领域的类似监管式数据采集方法；提出将真实运行中发现的安全相关驾驶场景存入场景库（scenario catalogue），并在OEM与安全监管机构间共享经验教训。

Result: ISMR被认定为应对ADS安全评估挑战的必要工具：可采集确认型式认证安全目标所需信息，沉淀安全相关场景供跨ADS类型复用分析，并支持行业与监管方知识共享。

Conclusion: 在用监测与报告（ISMR）是弥补传统验证方案不足、系统性应对ADS安全评估中未知因素的关键机制。

Abstract: European Union (EU) policymakers adopted revolutionary data collection provisions for Automated Driving Systems (ADS) in the recently approved regulation that allows driverless vehicles to be operated on public roads. The framework is inspired by best practices developed at the United Nations Economic Commission for Europe(UNECE) level: the In-Service Monitoring and Reporting (ISMR); and by similar operational data collection regulatory approaches in nuclear energy production and transportation fields. The collection of real-world data will enable the competent safety authorities to gather the information needed to confirm the homologation safety target. Safety-relevant driving scenarios discovered during the real-world operation of a given ADS can also be stored in a scenario catalogue to investigate how other ADS types might have addressed such a traffic conflict. Moreover, lessons learnt deriving from the data collected can be shared among original equipment manufacturers (OEMs) and safety authorities. Ultimately, the ISMR is recognised as a necessary tool to properly tackle the challenges associated with ADS safety assessment given the number of unknowns that might remain undisclosed by leveraging the traditional homologation validation scheme only.

</details>


### [101] [CAP: Continuously Adaptive Perception-Blind Humanoid Locomotion via Learned Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11553)
*Hongjin Chen,Zijun Xu,Shihao Ma,Yi Zhao,Xilai Liu,Ke Ma,Wei Zhang,Chunyang Xie,Pengfei Li,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAP 提出单阶段人形机器人运动策略：用感知世界模型编码器（作为学习式去噪器）从受损深度重建干净深度，并配合共激活本体感觉变分编码器提供无深度身体状态信息；训练时对世界模型输入施加深度噪声课程，对策略侧隐变量施加特征 dropout，使策略覆盖整个感知质量谱。仿真中在深度有效时匹配或超越感知基线，感知变差时比二值切换基线退化更平滑；Unitree G1 实机验证了间歇遮挡、真实传感器损坏与室外深度伪影下的鲁棒运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂地形上的人形运动需要前瞻性外部感知来预判障碍，但实机部署中该信号不可靠，会部分或间歇性失效。现有感知策略默认深度观测干净且同分布，而近期统一感知与盲控的方法多采用子策略路由或切换，未利用部分损坏深度中仍可恢复的信息。

Method: 单阶段策略 CAP：1）感知世界模型编码器，训练为学习式去噪器，从受损深度重建干净深度；2）共激活本体感觉变分编码器，提供无深度身体状态信息；3）耦合训练配方——世界模型输入端的深度噪声课程 + 面向策略的隐变量上的世界模型特征 dropout。

Result: 仿真中，深度有效时性能匹配或优于感知基线；感知恶化时退化比二值切换基线更平滑。Unitree G1 上受控试验与室内外部署展示了在间歇遮挡、真实传感器损坏、室外深度伪影下的感知鲁棒运动。

Conclusion: 将感知与盲控统一为单阶段策略，通过学习式去噪恢复部分受损深度并配合噪声课程与特征 dropout，可在全感知质量谱上实现平滑鲁棒的实机人形运动。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion across complex terrain demands forward-looking exteroception to anticipate obstacles, yet this signal is unreliable in real-world deployment, failing partially and intermittently. Existing perceptive policies often assume that depth observations remain clean and in-distribution, while recent attempts to unify perceptive and blind control typically route or switch between separate sub-policies, leaving recoverable information in partially corrupted depth unexploited. We instead propose CAP, a single-stage humanoid locomotion policy that recovers this signal with a perceptive world-model encoder trained as a learned denoiser to reconstruct clean depth from a corrupted input, together with a co-active proprioceptive variational encoder that supplies depth-free body-state information. A coupled training recipe pairs a depth-noise curriculum on the world-model input with world-model feature dropout on the policy-facing latent, exposing the policy to failures across the entire perception-quality spectrum. In simulation, CAP matches or improves upon perceptive baselines when depth remains informative, and degrades more smoothly than a binary-switching baseline as perception worsens. On the Unitree G1, controlled trials and indoor-outdoor deployments demonstrate perception-robust locomotion under intermittent occlusion, real-sensor corruption, and outdoor depth artifacts.

</details>


### [102] [Memory as Plans: World-Action Modeling with Memory-Grounded Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11561)
*Sizhe Zhao,Haozhe Xie,Weiyu Zhao,Chenchu Zhang,Huan Wang,Chenyang Wang,Qinglin Liu,Shengping Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MaP-WAM 将非马尔可夫长时程操作任务中的记忆建模拆分为"记忆驱动的规划"与"计划条件化执行"，用长时程多模态情节记忆作为规划证据，并以 World-Action-Progress 模型联合预测动作块与执行进度，实现固定执行器上下文与近恒定的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界操作任务往往是非马尔可夫的，需要超出当前观测的长时程记忆。已有记忆机制依赖语言摘要、增长的视觉窗口或其组合，会丢失细粒度视觉证据，或在历史覆盖与执行效率之间被迫权衡。作者希望避免让执行器反复以完整历史为条件，从而同时保留细粒度证据并保持推理效率。

Method: 提出 MaP-WAM（Memory-as-Plans）框架：将记忆表示为已完成片段记录（语言指令 + 稀疏视觉上下文），把情节记忆转换为紧凑计划（下一片段级语言计划 + 对应视觉引导）。由 World-Action-Progress (WAP) 模型在执行时联合预测动作块与执行进度，在未知时长下执行每个计划；通过计划-观测对齐校准预测进度，实现自适应片段切换与闭环上下文更新。执行器上下文长度固定，结构化注意力在规划与执行中均支持 KV 缓存。

Result: 在 RMBench 上取得 83.3% 成功率（SOTA），真实机器人任务达 78.0% 成功率，且随任务历史增长执行器推理延迟基本保持恒定。

Conclusion: 以"计划"作为记忆的抽象载体，可在不牺牲细粒度视觉证据的前提下解决长时程记忆的覆盖-效率权衡，并保持执行端的恒定上下文与恒定延迟，为长时程非马尔可夫操作提供可行范式。

Abstract: Mainstream robotic policies often adopt a Markovian formulation, but many complex real-world manipulation tasks are inherently non-Markovian, requiring long-horizon memory beyond the current observation. Existing memory mechanisms often rely on language summaries, growing visual windows, or their combinations, and may therefore lose fine-grained visual evidence or face a trade-off between history coverage and execution efficiency. We introduce MaP-WAM, a Memory-as-Plans framework that decomposes memory-dependent world-action modeling into memory-grounded planning and plan-conditioned execution, and uses long-term multimodal episodic context as planning-time evidence rather than repeatedly conditioning the executor on the full history. MaP-WAM represents memory as completed segment records containing language instructions and sparse visual context, and converts this episodic memory into compact plans comprising the next segment-level language plan and corresponding visual guidance. A World-Action-Progress (WAP) model executes each plan over an unknown duration by jointly predicting action chunks and corresponding execution progress at inference time, calibrating predicted progress through plan-observation alignment for adaptive segment transitions and closed-loop context updates. MaP-WAM keeps the executor context length fixed, while structured attention further enables key-value caching in both planning and execution. MaP-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on RMBench with an 83.3% success rate and attains 78.0% success on real-robot tasks, while maintaining approximately constant executor inference latency as task history grows.

</details>


### [103] [Quasi-static analysis of passive stability in a novel underactuated multi-finger hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11579)
*Léonie Plancoulaine,Sylvain Guégan,Franck Plestan,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出准静态解析方法评估欠驱动多指手的被动稳定性，并设计集成差动弹簧滑块机构的三指手，分析圆柱/球形抓取在不同物体尺寸下的稳定平衡位形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动手以较少驱动器实现自适应鲁棒抓取，但抓取物稳定平衡位形难以预测，缺乏解析层面的被动稳定性评估手段。

Method: 准静态解析建模；设计含差动弹簧-滑块机构的三指欠驱动手架构；针对圆柱与球形两类典型抓取，分析差动机构对整体抓取行为的影响及物体尺寸对稳定平衡构型的作用。

Result: 给出了差动机构作用下抓取行为的变化规律，以及物体尺寸与稳定平衡构型之间的解析关系（摘要未提供实机或数值验证细节）。

Conclusion: 差动机构是决定欠驱动手被动稳定抓取位形的关键因素，准静态解析方法可用于预测其稳定平衡状态。

Abstract: Underactuated robotic hands achieve adaptive and robust grasping with a reduced number of actuators, but predicting the stable equilibrium pose of the grasped object remains a significant challenge. This paper introduces a quasi-static analytical approach to assess passive stability in underactuated multi-finger hands. A novel three-finger hand architecture integrating a differential spring-loaded slider mechanism is introduced, enabling versatile and adaptive grasping. The study focuses on how the differential mechanism influences the overall grasp behavior and analyzes the effect of object size on the stable equilibrium configurations for two canonical grasp types: cylindrical and spherical.

</details>


### [104] [Contact-Aware Incremental Model Predictive Control for an Underactuated Aerial Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11661)
*Darwin Liu,Tamas Keviczky,Sihao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出接触感知的空中书写控制框架，结合NMPC做末端位置与法向力跟踪、全身INDI抗扰动，在欠驱动四旋翼单自由度刚性臂上实现五自由度位姿与接触力同步跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂在接触任务（如空中书写）中需在不完全驱动、无专用力/力矩传感器的条件下同时精确跟踪末端位姿与接触法向力，同时抵抗摩擦与气动扰动，而现有方案多依赖全驱动平台或复杂多自由度机械臂。

Method: 将非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）用于小参考穿透深度下的末端执行器位置与法向力精确跟踪，并与全身增量非线性动态逆（INDI）结合以增强接触过程中的摩擦与气动扰动鲁棒性；在基于四旋翼的空中机械臂（刚性、单连杆、1自由度臂）上开展仿真与实机验证。

Result: 空中书写实验覆盖垂直与倾斜表面、多种参考力、不同摩擦条件及风扰；结果表明在仅配备简单刚性单连杆臂的标准欠驱动四旋翼上，无需全驱动平台、复杂机械臂或专用力/力矩传感器，即可实现鲁棒的同步五自由度末端位姿与接触力跟踪。

Conclusion: 接触感知的NMPC+INDI组合可在欠驱动、硬件简化的空中操作平台上实现鲁棒的位姿-力同步跟踪，为低成本空中接触作业提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present a robust contact-aware control framework for aerial writing on an underactuated platform. The framework combines nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) for accurate end-effector position and normal-force tracking at small reference penetration depths, with consistent performance across controller tunings, with whole-body incremental nonlinear dynamic inversion (INDI) for robustness to frictional and aerodynamic disturbances during contact. The proposed controllers are validated on a quadrotor-based aerial manipulator with a rigid, single-link, one-degree-of-freedom (DoF) arm in simulation and real-world experiments. The aerial writing experiments span vertical and inclined surfaces, multiple reference forces, different friction conditions, and wind disturbances. The results demonstrate that robust simultaneous five-DoF end-effector pose and contact-force tracking is achievable on a standard underactuated quadrotor with a simple, rigid, single-link arm, without requiring a fully actuated platform, a complex arm, or dedicated force/torque sensing.

</details>


### [105] [ActSafeGuard: Differentiable and Training-Aligned Constraint Enforcement for Flow-Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11697)
*Jianming Ma,Rongjun Jin,Xiaxi Si,Yang Zhang,Yiheng Li,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActSafeGuard 提出一种可微、训练对齐的安全防护层，将硬动作可行性约束嵌入基于 flow-matching 的 VLA/WAM 策略训练中，通过解析式射线缩放算子实现边界感知梯度，在 π0.5 与 Fast-WAM 上实现 100% 逐步安全率且不损失任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 与 WAM 虽具备通用操作能力，但生成的动作用于部署时可能违反硬物理约束而不安全/不可行。现有安全方法要么只优化统计安全目标而缺乏逐步确定性保证，要么仅在推理阶段纠正动作，导致训练与执行之间存在错配。

Method: 提出 ActSafeGuard：针对 flow-matching 策略的可微、训练对齐安全防护层。核心是解析式射线缩放（ray-scaling）算子设计，使不安全动作沿射线方向缩放回可行域，并产生边界感知梯度，引导模型自然学习受限流形，而非在推理时外挂安全模块。方法具有最小侵入性，可叠加于多种基础骨干。

Result: 在 π0.5 与 Fast-WAM 两种标准基础骨干、多种任务上验证：ActSafeGuard 一致达到 100% step safety rate，同时完全保持甚至提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: 通过将硬可行性纳入策略学习，ActSafeGuard 为安全具身智能部署提供了可扩展、最小侵入的方案，兼顾逐步安全保证与任务性能，缓解了训练-执行错配问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World-Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet their generated actions may violate hard physical constraints and therefore be unsafe or infeasible for deployment. Existing safety approaches either optimize statistical safety objectives without deterministic per-step guarantees or correct unsafe actions only during inference, creating a mismatch between policy training and execution. We introduce ActSafeGuard, a differentiable and training-aligned safeguard layer for flow-matching based policies. ActSafeGuard integrates hard action feasibility into policy learning, not merely treating safety as an inference-time external component. Through an analytical ray-scaling operator design, ActSafeGuard enables boundary-aware gradients to guide the model to naturally learn constrained manifolds. Extensive experiments on multiple standard foundation backbones ($π_{0.5}$ and Fast-WAM) across various tasks demonstrate that ActSafeGuard consistently achieves a $100\%$ step safety rate while fully preserving or even boosting task success rates, providing a scalable and minimally invasive solution for safe embodied AI deployment.

</details>


### [106] [Aerodynamic Prior-Free Coordinated Trajectory Generation and Tracking Control for a Tail-Sitter UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11698)
*Erchao Rong,Zihao Liu,Junning Liang,Jianguo Wang,Xiao Jie,Haoran Fu,Ziliang Chen,Ximin Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出尾座式UAV全包线协调轨迹生成与跟踪控制框架，分阶段气动建模（φ理论解析微分平坦映射+局部简化模型用于预测控制在线辨识），无需机型专属气动先验，仿真与实机验证高精度跟踪，开源代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尾座式UAV在全包线飞行中气动高度非线性，传统控制依赖针对特定机型的风洞/系统辨识气动先验，成本高且泛化差；目标是免气动辨识先验地实现全包线轨迹跟踪。

Method: 按规划与跟踪的功能差异采用阶段特异气动建模：规划侧利用协调飞行下的φ理论推导解析微分平坦映射生成可行轨迹；跟踪侧建立局部精确的简化模型用于预测控制，实现在线气动参数估计，形成轨迹生成与控制协同框架。

Result: 仿真与轻风条件下实机飞行测试广泛验证，在测试的气动条件下展现出高精度跟踪与适应性；据作者所述为首个在无气动辨识流程下完成尾座式UAV全包线测试区间精确轨迹跟踪的实机演示。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 分阶段气动建模可替代机型专属气动先验，为全包线尾座式UAV提供可用的协调规划-控制方案，具备实机可行性与开源复现基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a coordinated trajectory generation and tracking control framework for a tail-sitter unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV), which does not require aerodynamic priors identified for a specific airframe while addressing the challenge of flight control under highly nonlinear aerodynamics across the full flight envelope. The core innovation lies in employing phase-specific aerodynamic modeling strategies for planning and tracking, tailored to their distinct functional characteristics, without requiring airframe-specific aerodynamic priors. Specifically, the phi-theory model under coordinated flight is employed to derive an analytic differential flatness mapping, and a simplified but locally accurate model is established for predictive control to enable real-time aerodynamic parameter estimation. The proposed framework is evaluated extensively through both simulation and challenging real-world flight tests under mild wind conditions, showing high-precision tracking and adaptability across the tested aerodynamic conditions.
